📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: BMC Bioinformatics  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-09  |  DOI: 10.1186/s12859-026-06579-w  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

ESM-embedR:一种蛋白质语言模型框架,用于比较 突变 分析以及 Lassa 和 埃博拉病毒 序列的计算非典型性评分

ESM-embedR: a protein language model framework for comparative mutation analysis and computational atypicality scoring of Lassa and Ebola virus sequences
作者： Olatunji Matthew Kolawole, Damilola M. Olayemi, Damilare I. Taiwo, Caroline F. Kolawole, George E. Ejembi
摘要中文翻译具有大流行潜力的病毒病原体的出现和重新出现需要强大的计算框架,能够解释进化和功能环境中的序列变异。拉沙病毒 (LASV) 和 埃博拉病毒 (EBOV) 分别是沙粒病毒科和丝状病毒科的两个成员,代表着截然不同的进化范式:LASV 在整个西非表现出巨大的遗传多样性,而 EBOV 暴发 历史上的遗传变异更为有限。了解控制这些病毒的差异突变限制为 监测、疫苗 设计和治疗开发提供了重要的见解。这项研究提出了一个全面的、端到端的计算框架,将比较病毒学和实际部署联系起来。分析涵盖 2,502 个糖蛋白 (GP) 序列(780 个 LASV GP、1722 个 EBOV GP),这些序列源自 NCBI GenBank,涵盖所有记录的 EBOV 暴发 (1976-2024) 和精选的 LASV 存储库。通过参考引导比对 (MAFFT) 从完整的 基因组 组件中提取 EBOV GP,从而实现直接的蛋白质范围匹配比较。该框架通过保护和熵分析与替代负担量化整合了与 MAFFT 一致的位点级约束分类,以表征突变景观。使用 ESM-2 的蛋白质语言模型嵌入空间表征通过质心几何、异常值检测和 PCA 可视化提供补充的描述性见解。对于监督分类和部署,该框架采用轻量级、可解释的组成特征(序列长度和氨基酸频率)而不是 ESM-2 嵌入,这是一种优先考虑部署效率和可解释性的有意设计选择。该分类器与计算非典型性评分层相结合,该层可量化与训练群体质心的序列偏差。比较分析揭示了突变结构中深刻的不对称性。EBOV GP 表现出近乎完全的位置限制,其中 94.7% 的比对位点被分类为关键位点,5.3% 被分类为保守位点,而 LASV GP 显示出极大的灵活性,其中 92.0% 为热点位点,5.3% 为中间位点,2.2% 为保守位点,0.5% 为关键位点。ESM-2 嵌入分析证实了这些发现。逻辑回归分类器在 GP 匹配的 队列研究 上实现了完美的保留性能(准确率 1.000、精度 1.000、召回率 1.000、F1 1.000、ROC-AUC 1.000),反映了强大的类可分离性,同时承认在没有明确验证的情况下不能假设这种性能推广到所有独立的 EBOV 或 LASV 变体。该模型与可解释的非典型性层集成,生成具有有限解释带、非典型性 z 分数和自然语言解释的计算非典型性指数,通过公共 Streamlit 应用程序部署。这项工作的新颖性在于将已建立的方法(位点级约束分析、替代负担量化、蛋白质语言模型嵌入表征、可解释分类和非典型性评分)集成到单个可重复、可公开部署的管道中,用于比较 LASV-EBOV 序列分析。每种方法都有单独的先例;它们在一个统一的框架内进行综合,将静态的比较见解转化为动态的、可访问的序列分析能力,这代表了主要的进步。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对强大的计算工具的需求,以解释具有大流行潜力的病毒病原体的序列变异,特别关注 埃博拉病毒 (EBOV) 和拉沙病毒 (LASV) 作为对比的进化范式。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2,502 个糖蛋白 (GP) 序列,其中包括来自 NCBI GenBank 的 1,722 个 EBOV GP 序列,涵盖 1976 年至 2024 年所有记录的 EBOV 暴发,使用参考引导比对 (MAFFT) 从完整的 基因组 组件中提取 GP。
核心方法
该框架通过守恒和熵分析与替代负担量化集成了与 MAFFT 一致的位点级约束分类,并使用 ESM-2 蛋白质语言模型嵌入来表征嵌入空间,包括质心几何和 PCA 可视化。
主要结果
EBOV GP 表现出近乎完全的位置限制,其中 94.7% 的比对位点被分类为关键位点,5.3% 被分类为保守位点,而 LASV GP 显示出极大的灵活性,其中 92.0% 为热点位点,5.3% 为中间位点,2.2% 为保守位点,0.5% 为关键位点。
解释与创新
作者将 EBOV 和 LASV 突变结构中深刻的不对称性解释为差异进化限制的证据,新颖之处在于将现有方法整合到一个统一的、可重复的管道中以进行比较序列分析。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过强调 EBOV 和 LASV 的对比突变景观,特别是 EBOV GP 的受限 进化,为 监测 策略和 疫苗 或治疗设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认,在没有明确验证的情况下,完美的分类器性能(准确度 1.000、精确度 1.000、召回率 1.000、F1 1.000、ROC-AUC 1.000)可能无法推广到所有独立的 EBOV 或 LASV 变体,这表明证据强度中等。

ESM-embedR: a protein language model framework for comparative mutation analysis and computational atypicality scoring of Lassa and Ebola virus sequences

Authors: Olatunji Matthew Kolawole, Damilola M. Olayemi, Damilare I. Taiwo, Caroline F. Kolawole, George E. Ejembi
Original abstract (English)
The emergence and re-emergence of viral pathogens with pandemic potential necessitate robust computational frameworks capable of interpreting sequence variation in evolutionary and functional contexts. Lassa virus (LASV) and Ebola virus (EBOV), two members of the Arenaviridae and Filoviridae families, respectively, represent contrasting evolutionary paradigms: LASV exhibits substantial genetic diversity across West Africa, while EBOV outbreaks have historically been characterised by more constrained genetic variation. Understanding the differential mutational constraints governing these viruses provides critical insights for surveillance, vaccine design, and therapeutic development. This study presents a comprehensive, end-to-end computational framework bridging comparative virology and practical deployment. Analysis encompassed 2,502 glycoprotein (GP) sequences (780 LASV GP, 1722 EBOV GP) sourced from NCBI GenBank across all documented EBOV outbreaks (1976–2024) and curated LASV repositories. EBOV GP was extracted from full-genome assemblies via reference-guided alignment (MAFFT), enabling a direct, protein-scope-matched comparison. The framework integrates MAFFT-aligned site-level constraint categorisation through conservation and entropy analysis with substitution burden quantification to characterise the mutational landscape. Protein language model embedding-space characterisation using ESM-2 provides complementary descriptive insight through centroid geometry, outlier detection, and PCA visualisation. For supervised classification and deployment, the framework employs lightweight, interpretable composition features (sequence length and amino-acid frequencies) rather than ESM-2 embeddings, an intentional design choice that prioritises deployment efficiency and interpretability. The classifier is coupled with a computational atypicality scoring layer that quantifies sequence deviation from training-population centroids. The comparative analysis revealed profound asymmetries in mutational architecture. EBOV GP exhibited near-complete positional constraint with 94.7% of aligned sites classified as Critical and 5.3% as Conserved, while LASV GP displayed substantial flexibility with 92.0% Hotspots, 5.3% Intermediate sites, 2.2% Conserved, and 0.5% Critical. ESM-2 embedding analysis corroborated these findings. A logistic regression classifier achieved perfect held-out performance (accuracy 1.000, precision 1.000, recall 1.000, F1 1.000, ROC-AUC 1.000) on the GP-matched cohort, reflecting robust class separability while acknowledging that this performance cannot be assumed to generalise to all independent EBOV or LASV variants without explicit validation. This model was integrated with an interpretable atypicality layer generating a computational atypicality index with bounded interpretive bands, atypicality z-scores, and natural language interpretations, deployed via a public Streamlit application. The novelty of this work lies in the integration of established methods (site-level constraint analysis, substitution-burden quantification, protein language model embedding characterization, interpretable classification, and atypicality scoring) into a single reproducible, openly deployable pipeline for comparative LASV-EBOV sequence analysis. Each individual method has precedents in isolation; their synthesis within a unified framework that transforms static comparative insights into dynamic, accessible sequence-analysis capabilities represents the principal advance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for robust computational tools to interpret sequence variation in viral pathogens with pandemic potential, specifically focusing on Ebola virus (EBOV) and Lassa virus (LASV) as contrasting evolutionary paradigms.
Design and population
The study analysed 2,502 glycoprotein (GP) sequences, including 1,722 EBOV GP sequences sourced from NCBI GenBank across all documented EBOV outbreaks from 1976 to 2024, using reference-guided alignment (MAFFT) to extract GP from full-genome assemblies.
Core methods
The framework integrated MAFFT-aligned site-level constraint categorisation through conservation and entropy analysis with substitution burden quantification, and used ESM-2 protein language model embeddings for embedding-space characterisation, including centroid geometry and PCA visualisation.
Main results
EBOV GP exhibited near-complete positional constraint with 94.7% of aligned sites classified as Critical and 5.3% as Conserved, while LASV GP displayed substantial flexibility with 92.0% Hotspots, 5.3% Intermediate sites, 2.2% Conserved, and 0.5% Critical.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the profound asymmetries in mutational architecture between EBOV and LASV as evidence of differential evolutionary constraints, and the novelty lies in integrating established methods into a unified, reproducible pipeline for comparative sequence analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance strategies and vaccine or therapeutic design by highlighting the contrasting mutational landscapes of EBOV and LASV, particularly the constrained evolution of EBOV GP.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that the perfect classifier performance (accuracy 1.000, precision 1.000, recall 1.000, F1 1.000, ROC-AUC 1.000) may not generalise to all independent EBOV or LASV variants without explicit validation, indicating a moderate strength of evidence.

诊断和 疫苗 研发投资不足,使世界在 2026 年面临本迪布焦病毒流行的更大风险

Underinvestment in diagnostic and vaccine research & development has put the world at greater risk from the Bundibugyo virus epidemic in 2026
作者： Hinh Ly
摘要中文翻译2026 年 5 月 16 日,世界卫生组织 (WHO) 宣布刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 和乌干达本迪布焦 埃博拉病毒 (BDBV) 目前的 暴发 为突发公共卫生事件 (ht...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究强调,由于诊断和 疫苗 研发投资不足,本迪布焦病毒流行的风险增加。作者认为,缺乏投资阻碍了对 暴发 的有效公共卫生反应。
设计与对象
该研究是对刚果民主共和国和乌干达本迪布焦病毒流行的编辑分析,重点关注世界卫生组织于 2026 年 5 月 16 日宣布的公共卫生紧急状态。它探讨了诊断和 疫苗 研究投资不足对管理此类 暴发 的更广泛影响。
核心方法
这些方法包括审查公共卫生紧急状态声明和分析全球对本迪布焦病毒 暴发 的反应,强调诊断和 疫苗 研究投资的缺乏是核心焦点。
主要结果
主要结果表明,世界卫生组织于2026年5月16日宣布本迪布焦病毒暴发为突发公共卫生事件,凸显了该流行病的严重性和潜在的全球影响。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明迫切需要增加对 埃博拉病毒(特别是本迪布焦病毒)的诊断和 疫苗 研究的投资,以预防未来的流行病。这是对全球卫生政策采取行动的新颖呼吁。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对公共卫生政策具有重大影响,强调优先考虑本迪布焦病毒的诊断和 疫苗 研究的重要性,以减轻未来的 疫苗 影响并提高全球防范能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单一来源,即世界卫生组织的声明,不包括详细的统计或实验数据。然而,鉴于官方承认公共卫生紧急情况,证据很充分。

Underinvestment in diagnostic and vaccine research & development has put the world at greater risk from the Bundibugyo virus epidemic in 2026

Authors: Hinh Ly
Original abstract (English)
On 16 May 2026, the World Health Organization (WHO) declared the current outbreak of Bundibugyo Ebola virus (BDBV) in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) and Uganda a public health emergency (ht...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study highlights the increased risk of a Bundibugyo virus epidemic due to underinvestment in diagnostic and vaccine research and development. The authors argue that this lack of investment has hindered effective public health responses to the outbreak.
Design and population
The study is an editorial analysis of the Bundibugyo virus epidemic in the Democratic Republic of Congo and Uganda, focusing on the public health emergency declared by the World Health Organization on 16 May 2026. It examines the broader implications of underinvestment in diagnostic and vaccine research for managing such outbreaks.
Core methods
The methods involve a review of the public health emergency declaration and an analysis of the global response to the Bundibugyo virus outbreak, emphasizing the lack of investment in diagnostic and vaccine research as the central focus.
Main results
The main results indicate that the Bundibugyo virus outbreak was declared a public health emergency by the World Health Organization on 16 May 2026, underscoring the severity and potential global impact of the epidemic.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence of the critical need for increased investment in diagnostic and vaccine research for Ebola viruses, particularly Bundibugyo virus, to prevent future epidemics. This is a novel call to action for global health policy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have significant implications for public health policy, emphasizing the importance of prioritizing diagnostic and vaccine research for Bundibugyo virus to mitigate future outbreaks and improve global preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single source, the World Health Organization's declaration, and does not include detailed statistical or experimental data. However, the evidence is strong given the official recognition of the public health emergency.

Journal: The lancet. HIV  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-07  |  DOI: 10.1016/s2352-3018(26)00118-9  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, S2352-3018(26)00118-9
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

一剂或两剂 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP 埃博拉病毒 疫苗 在成人和青少年 HIV 感染者中的安全性和免疫原性:一项 2 期、多中心、双盲、随机、安慰剂对照试验。

Safety and immunogenicity of one or two doses of the rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP Ebola virus vaccine in adults and adolescents living with HIV: a phase 2, multicentre, double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled trial.
作者： Cécile Tremblay, Innocent Valea, Birahim Pierre Ndiaye, Houreratou Barry, May ElSherif, Curtis Cooper, Janis L Breeze, Yun Cai, Lingyun Ye, Tosin Omole
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃博拉病毒病 暴发 需要有效的 疫苗接种 策略,但缺乏 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP 疫苗 在 HIV 感染者中的免疫原性和安全性数据。该研究通过评估 疫苗 是否可以引发保护性 免疫应答 并在接受抗逆转录病毒治疗的成人和青少年艾滋病毒感染者中保持安全,解决了一个关键的知识差距。
设计与对象
这项 2 期、多中心、双盲、随机、安慰剂对照试验招募了 251 名 13-70 岁的参与者,这些参与者经实验室确诊为 HIV 感染,并且在 ART 上检测不到病毒载量,根据 CD4 计数和年龄分为 5 个 队列研究。参与者以 4:1 的比例随机分配接受一剂 (队列研究 1-4) 或两剂相隔 56 天 (队列研究 5) 的 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP 或安慰剂,并进行盲分配并随访至第 365 天。
核心方法
通过糖蛋白 ELISA (GP-ELISA) 和中和 (PRNT) 在符合方案人群中测量免疫原性,主要终点为 队列研究 5 给药后第 28 天和第二次给药后 28 天。安全终点包括在接受治疗的人群中评估的主动和主动不良事件、实验室异常和直至第 365 天的严重不良事件。
主要结果
One dose of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP elicited a >40-fold increase in GP-ELISA antibodies (GMTR 42.90, 95% CI 31.49–58.43; p<0.0001) and an 11.51-fold GMFI in PRNT titres at day 28. A two-dose regimen in cohort 5 produced a >200-fold higher GP-ELISA response (GMTR 208.66, 95% CI 115.57–376.72; p<0.0001) and an 86.64-fold GMFI in PRNT titres 28 days post-second dose.
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,单剂量的 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP 对于接受 ART 且病毒载量无法检测到的 HIV 感染者具有高度免疫原性和良好的耐受性,而两剂量方案可显着增强反应而不增加反应原性。该试验提供了针对这一待研究人群的新的免疫原性和安全性数据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在埃博拉 暴发 期间在 HIV 感染者中使用 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP,为高危人群的 疫苗接种 政策提供信息。可接受的安全性和强免疫原性表明在流行地区的实际部署潜力。
局限与证据强度
由于排除了可检测到 HIV 病毒载量或 CD4 计数较低的参与者,并且缺乏长期疗效数据,该研究的普遍性受到限制。尽管具有强大的免疫原性,但适度的样本量和单次试验设置值得谨慎解释,从而产生中等的总体证据强度。

Safety and immunogenicity of one or two doses of the rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP Ebola virus vaccine in adults and adolescents living with HIV: a phase 2, multicentre, double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled trial.

Authors: Cécile Tremblay, Innocent Valea, Birahim Pierre Ndiaye, Houreratou Barry, May ElSherif, Curtis Cooper, Janis L Breeze, Yun Cai, Lingyun Ye, Tosin Omole
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: A single dose of the replication-competent rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP vaccine is recommended by the WHO Strategic Advisory Group of Experts on Immunization during Ebola virus disease outbreaks. We aimed to assess the immunogenicity, safety, and tolerability of this vaccine in adults and adolescents with HIV. METHODS: In this phase 2, multicentre, double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled trial, we enrolled participants aged 13-70 years who had laboratory-confirmed HIV infection, were on antiretroviral therapy (ART), had an undetectable viral load ( 350 and <500 cells per μL), cohort 3 (adults with a CD4 count of 200-350 cells per μL), cohort 4 (adolescents [age 13-17 years] with a CD4 count ≥200 cells per μL), and cohort 5 (adults and adolescents with a CD4 count ≥200 cells per μL). Participants were randomly assigned 4:1 to receive one dose (cohorts 1-4) or two doses (56 days apart; cohort 5) of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP or placebo, administered at day 0. An unmasked staff member at each site prepared the vaccine or placebo; all other study personnel and participants were masked to group assignments. Participants were followed up to day 365. The primary immunogenicity endpoints, assessed in the per-protocol population, were Ebola virus-specific antibody responses measured by glycoprotein ELISA (GP-ELISA; geometric mean titre ratio [GMTR]) and neutralisation (geometric mean fold increase [GMFI] of plaque reduction neutralisation test [PRNT]) on day 28 in cohorts 1-5 combined (combined cohort), and 28 days after the second dose of vaccine or placebo in cohort 5. The primary safety endpoints, assessed in the as-treated population, were: the occurrence of each solicited local and systemic adverse event during a 14-day follow-up period after each vaccination; fever, joint pain (arthralgia), joint swelling (arthritis), rash, and blisters or vesicular lesions during a 42-day follow-up period after vaccination; any haematological and biochemical laboratory abnormality at 0, 3, 7, 14, and 28 days after each vaccine; occurrence of any unsolicited adverse event during a 42-day follow-up period after each vaccination; and occurrence of vaccine-related serious adverse events up to day 365 of the study. The trial is registered at ClinicalTrials.gov (NCT03031912) and is complete. FINDINGS: Between Aug 1, 2017, and March 3, 2022, we assessed 647 individuals for eligibility, 251 of whom were enrolled. 120 (48%) participants were male, 130 (52%) were female, 225 (90%) were Black (Black African or African Canadian), and median age was 52·0 years (IQR 48·0-56·0). 51 participants were enrolled in cohort 1, 50 in cohort 2, 50 in cohort 3, 50 in cohort 4, and 50 in cohort 5. 202 (80%) participants were assigned to receive rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP and 49 (20%) were assigned to receive placebo. One dose of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP elicited superior immune responses compared with placebo, with GP-ELISA antibodies in the combined cohort (per-protocol population) increasing over 40-fold (GMTR 42·90, 95% CI 31·49-58·43; p<0·0001). In cohort 5, the GP-ELISA geometric mean titre 28 days after the second dose was over 200-fold higher in the rVSV-ZEBOV-GP group than in the placebo group (GMTR 208·66, 95% CI 115·57-376·72; p<0·0001). GFMI of PRNT titres was 11·51 (95% CI 9·82-13·50) at day 28 for participants in the combined cohort who received one dose of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP and 86·64 (95% CI 69·85-107·46) 28 days after a second dose in cohort 5. Solicited adverse events up to 42 days after vaccination were mild to moderate in severity and transient. In the 14 days after the first dose of vaccine, the most common adverse events were injection site pain (130 of 201 participants, 65%), headache (109 of 201, 54%), fatigue (91 of 201, 45%), and joint pain (50 of 201, 25%). Unsolicited adverse events to day 42 were not different between participants who received rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP (103 of 201, 51%) and those who received placebo (22 of 49, 45%; p=0·52). No clinically significant changes in CD4 cell counts, HIV viral load, or haematological and biochemical laboratory tests were observed. INTERPRETATION: A single dose of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP is highly immunogenic in people living with HIV on ART with an undetectable viral load, with a tolerable safety profile. A two-dose regimen in a subset of participating adolescents and adults showed a significant boosting at day 28, with no increase in reactogenicity compared with the first dose. FUNDING: International Development Research Centre, US Department of Health and Human Services, Administration for Strategic Preparedness and Response, Biomedical Advanced Research and Development Authority, and the Coalition for Epidemic Preparedness. TRANSLATION: For the French translation of the abstract see Supplementary Materials section.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Ebola virus disease outbreaks necessitate effective vaccination strategies, yet immunogenicity and safety data for the rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP vaccine in people living with HIV were lacking. The study addressed a critical knowledge gap by evaluating whether this vaccine could elicit protective immune responses and remain safe in adults and adolescents with HIV on antiretroviral therapy.
Design and population
This phase 2, multicentre, double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled trial enrolled 251 participants aged 13–70 years with laboratory-confirmed HIV infection and undetectable viral loads on ART, stratified by CD4 count and age into five cohorts. Participants were randomly assigned 4:1 to receive one dose (cohorts 1–4) or two doses 56 days apart (cohort 5) of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP or placebo, with masked assignments and follow-up to day 365.
Core methods
Immunogenicity was measured by glycoprotein ELISA (GP-ELISA) and neutralisation (PRNT) in the per-protocol population, with primary endpoints at day 28 post-dose and 28 days after the second dose in cohort 5. Safety endpoints included solicited and unsolicited adverse events, laboratory abnormalities, and serious adverse events up to day 365, assessed in the as-treated population.
Main results
One dose of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP elicited a >40-fold increase in GP-ELISA antibodies (GMTR 42.90, 95% CI 31.49–58.43; p<0.0001) and an 11.51-fold GMFI in PRNT titres at day 28. A two-dose regimen in cohort 5 produced a >200-fold higher GP-ELISA response (GMTR 208.66, 95% CI 115.57–376.72; p<0.0001) and an 86.64-fold GMFI in PRNT titres 28 days post-second dose.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that a single dose of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP is highly immunogenic and well-tolerated in people living with HIV on ART with undetectable viral loads, with a two-dose regimen significantly boosting responses without increased reactogenicity. This trial provides novel immunogenicity and safety data specific to this understudied population.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the use of rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP in HIV-infected individuals during Ebola outbreaks, informing vaccination policies for a high-risk group. The tolerable safety profile and strong immunogenicity suggest practical deployment potential in endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s generalisability is limited by the exclusion of participants with detectable HIV viral loads or low CD4 counts, and the absence of long-term efficacy data. Despite robust immunogenicity, the moderate sample size and single-trial setting warrant cautious interpretation, yielding a moderate overall evidence strength.

一项基于专家参与的风险评估,旨在为喀麦隆的人畜共患疾病 监测 提供信息:埃博拉病毒病 和裂谷热的案例

A participatory expert-based risk assessment to inform zoonotic disease surveillance in Cameroon: the case of Ebola virus disease and Rift Valley fever
作者： Inès Sopbué Kamguem, Nathalie Kirschvink, Nicolas Antoine-Moussiaux, Florence De Longueville, Cécile Ingrid Djuikoue, Noel K. Joseph, Sophie Molia, Abel Wade, Catherine Linard, Nicolas Antoine‐Moussiaux
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景病毒性出血热(VHFs)是一组由RNA病毒引起的人畜共患疾病,病原体包括埃博拉病毒、马尔堡病毒、拉沙病毒、裂谷热病毒等。此前的研究已将喀麦隆确定为高风险国家,原因是其有利的生态气候条件以及易感哺乳动物和 媒介 的存在,这些哺乳动物和 媒介 已被证实对引起裂谷热 (RVF) 和 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 的病毒呈阳性。尽管有证据表明裂谷热病毒在喀麦隆动物中传播以及人类对埃博拉相关病毒有血清学反应,但该国尚未正式记录裂谷热或埃博拉病毒病的流行情况。本研究的目的是提供关于喀麦隆裂谷热和埃博拉病毒病出现的感知风险因素和时间模式的新颖、具体的知识,喀麦隆关于这两种疾病的流行病学数据有限,通过牲畜成分及其在动物宿主中的早期传播动态和人畜共患埃博拉病毒病中的人畜共患跨物种溢出来探索裂谷热。方法 目前的工作提出通过开发 One Health 综合风险评估工具来识别可感知的高风险期和关键风险因素,对裂谷热和埃博拉病毒病的出现进行风险评估。采用混合、半定量方法,包括专家半结构化访谈和月历来收集数据。然后将基于专家的风险评估与科学文献中得出的风险因素进行比较。结果 对于 RVF,最潮湿的月份似乎风险最高,牲畜迁移期间动物间的 传播 增加。专家确定的关键空间决定因素包括降雨量、宿主 密度、储存宿主、媒介 和牲畜迁徙。对于埃博拉病毒病 跨物种溢出 风险,人类活动和行为、土地覆盖和 宿主 密度也被认为至关重要。观察到基于专家和基于文献的裂谷热和埃博拉病毒病风险因素的趋同和分歧,分别与生计和森林生存相关的牲畜贸易和社会文化实践存在差异。这种区别可能归因于两种权重来源分别采用不同的情境化和概括方法。结论 这项研究表明,即使在没有疾病史记录的地区,结合专家知识和文献证据的综合方法也能实现有意义的感知风险评估,生成新颖且针对具体情况的信息,并支持完善预防策略。引入的参与式视觉工具可以识别和量化假定的人畜共患风险因素及其时间动态,为风险绘图、管理和沟通提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过提供关于感知风险因素及其出现时间模式的新颖、具体的知识,解决喀麦隆 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 和裂谷热 (RVF) 的有限流行病学数据问题。
设计与对象
这项研究在喀麦隆进行了一项由专家参与的风险评估,重点关注该国由于其生态气候条件以及易感哺乳动物和 媒介 的存在而处于高风险状态。该研究涉及专家半结构化访谈和月度日历,以收集有关裂谷热和埃博拉病毒病风险因素的数据。
核心方法
使用了混合的半定量方法,整合了专家半结构化访谈和数据收集的月度日历。该研究开发了一种 One Health 综合风险评估工具,以识别感知的高风险期和关键风险因素。
主要结果
对于裂谷热,最潮湿的月份被认为风险最高,牲畜转移期间动物间的 传播 增加。对于埃博拉病毒病,人类活动和行为、土地覆盖和 宿主 密度被视为关键风险因素。
解释与创新
该研究强调,即使在没有疾病史记录的地区,基于专家的风险评估也可以通过将当地知识与科学文献相结合来生成新颖且针对具体情况的信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持完善喀麦隆人畜共患疾病的预防策略和风险图,提供对裂谷热和埃博拉病毒病出现时间动态的见解,从而为 监测 和管理实践提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究对专家意见和半定量方法的依赖可能会限制研究结果的普遍性,并且由于缺乏关于疾病 暴发 的直接经验数据,证据被认为是中等的。

A participatory expert-based risk assessment to inform zoonotic disease surveillance in Cameroon: the case of Ebola virus disease and Rift Valley fever

Authors: Inès Sopbué Kamguem, Nathalie Kirschvink, Nicolas Antoine-Moussiaux, Florence De Longueville, Cécile Ingrid Djuikoue, Noel K. Joseph, Sophie Molia, Abel Wade, Catherine Linard, Nicolas Antoine‐Moussiaux
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Viral haemorrhagic fevers (VHFs) are a group of zoonotic diseases caused by RNA viruses, including pathogens such as Ebola virus, Marburg virus, Lassa virus, and Rift Valley fever virus. Previous studies have identified Cameroon as a high-risk country, due to favourable ecoclimatic conditions and the presence of susceptible mammals and vectors that have been confirmed positive for viruses causing Rift Valley fever (RVF) and Ebola virus disease (EVD). Although evidence exists of RVF virus circulation in animals and serological reactivity to Ebola-related viruses among humans in Cameroon, the country has not officially documented epidemics of RVF or EVD. The objective of this study is to provide novel, context-refined knowledge of perceived risk factors and temporal patterns of RVF and EVD emergence in Cameroon, where epidemiological data on these two diseases are limited, with RVF explored through the livestock component and its early transmission dynamics in animal hosts, and zoonotic spillover to humans for EVD. Methods The present work proposes a risk assessment of RVF and EVD emergence through the development of a One Health integrated risk assessment tool for identifying perceived high-risk periods and key risk factors. A mixed, semi-quantitative approach involving expert semi-structured interviews, and a monthly calendar was used for data collection. Expert-based risk evaluation was then compared to risk factors derived from the scientific literature. Results For RVF, the wettest months seem to pose the highest risk, with increased inter-animal transmission during livestock transhumance. Key spatial determinants identified by experts included rainfall, host density, reservoirs, vectors, and livestock movements. For EVD spillover risk, human activities and behaviours, land cover, and host density were also perceived as critical. Convergences and divergences regarding expert-based and literature-based risk factors for RVF and EVD were observed, with variations concerning respectively livestock trade and socio-cultural practices linked to livelihood and forest-based subsistence. The distinction is likely attributable to the differing approaches of contextualization and generalization employed respectively by the two sources of weighting. Conclusions This study shows that, even in regions with no documented disease history, an integrated approach combining expert knowledge and literature-based evidence enables meaningful perceived risk assessment, generates novel and context-specific information, and supports the refinement of preventive strategies. The participatory visual tool introduced allows identification and quantification of putative zoonotic risk factors and their temporal dynamics, informing risk mapping, management, and communication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limited epidemiological data on Ebola virus disease (EVD) and Rift Valley fever (RVF) in Cameroon by providing novel, context-refined knowledge of perceived risk factors and temporal patterns of their emergence.
Design and population
This study conducted a participatory expert-based risk assessment in Cameroon, focusing on the country's high-risk status due to its ecoclimatic conditions and the presence of susceptible mammals and vectors. The study involved expert semi-structured interviews and a monthly calendar to collect data on risk factors for RVF and EVD.
Core methods
A mixed, semi-quantitative approach was used, integrating expert semi-structured interviews and a monthly calendar for data collection. The study developed a One Health integrated risk assessment tool to identify perceived high-risk periods and key risk factors.
Main results
For RVF, the wettest months were identified as posing the highest risk, with increased inter-animal transmission during livestock transhumance. For EVD, human activities and behaviours, land cover, and host density were perceived as critical risk factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that expert-based risk evaluation can generate novel and context-specific information, even in regions without documented disease history, by combining local knowledge with scientific literature.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the refinement of preventive strategies and risk mapping for zoonotic diseases in Cameroon, offering insights into the temporal dynamics of RVF and EVD emergence that can inform surveillance and management practices.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on expert opinion and semi-quantitative methods may limit the generalizability of findings, and the evidence is considered moderate due to the absence of direct empirical data on disease outbreaks.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

泛埃博拉病毒纳米颗粒 疫苗 可以保护啮齿动物免受扎伊尔病毒和苏丹病毒的致命感染

Pan-Ebolavirus nanoparticle vaccine provides protection in rodents from lethal infection by Zaire and Sudan viruses
作者： Connor Weidle, Natalie Brunette, Samuel P. Wrenn, Brooke Fiala, Rashmi Ravichandran, Kenneth D. Carr, Samantha E. Zak, Elizabeth E. Zumbrun, Russell R. Bakken, Michael Murphy
摘要中文翻译摘要 扎伊尔埃博拉病毒(EBOV)和苏丹埃博拉病毒(SUDV)都是埃博拉病毒属的成员,引起暴发,其特点是死亡率高,并且在动物储存宿主中重复出现暴发。丝状病毒糖蛋白(GP)是中和抗体的主要靶标,并构成当前 疫苗 的基础。在这里,我们描述了双组分自组装二十面体 I53-50 纳米粒子的设计、结构表征和评估,这些纳米粒子显示融合前 EBOV 或 SUDV GP 抗原,无论是单独的还是以鸡尾酒和镶嵌多价形式。EBOV-GP-I53-50 和 SUDV-GP-I53-50 纳米颗粒在小鼠和豚鼠中引起了强烈的同源保护。在小鼠适应的 EBOV 模型 (maEBOV) 中,镶嵌和鸡尾酒制剂的存活率低于匹配的 EBOV-GP-I53-50 GP 免疫原。相比之下,在 gpaSUDV 豚鼠模型 (gpSUDV) 中,混合物和镶嵌纳米粒子引发了针对 gpSUDV 的强大保护,并对 SUDV 和 EBOV GP 产生了可检测的抗体反应。这些发现表明,多价 GP-I53-50 纳米颗粒免疫原可以在相关模型中保护啮齿动物免于死亡、严重的临床疾病症状和体重减轻。加上 I53-50 平台已确立的临床安全性,这些结果支持继续努力开发泛埃博拉病毒 疫苗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
扎伊尔埃博拉病毒 (EBOV) 和苏丹埃博拉病毒 (SUDV) 导致 暴发 的死亡率很高,并且动物 储存宿主 重复出现 跨物种溢出,这凸显了对 疫苗 具有广泛保护性的需求。该研究探讨了展示 EBOV 和 SUDV 糖蛋白的多价纳米颗粒免疫原是否可以在相关动物模型中引发针对致命感染的保护性免疫。
设计与对象
该研究评估了显示融合前 EBOV 或 SUDV GP 抗原的双组分自组装二十面体 I53-50 纳米颗粒,无论是单独的、鸡尾酒的还是镶嵌多价形式。在小鼠适应的 EBOV (maEBOV) 和豚鼠适应的 SUDV (gpSUDV) 模型中评估了保护作用。
核心方法
对纳米颗粒进行了设计、结构表征,并评估了其在啮齿类动物中的免疫原性和保护作用。该研究报告了 maEBOV 或 gpSUDV 致死攻击后的生存率、临床症状和体重减轻结果。
主要结果
EBOV-GP-I53-50 和 SUDV-GP-I53-50 纳米颗粒在小鼠和豚鼠中引起了强烈的同源保护。在 maEBOV 模型中,镶嵌和鸡尾酒制剂的存活率低于匹配的 EBOV-GP-I53-50 免疫原。在 gpSUDV 模型中,混合物和镶嵌纳米粒子引发了针对 gpSUDV 的强大保护,并对 SUDV 和 EBOV GP 产生了可检测的抗体反应。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,多价 GP-I53-50 纳米颗粒免疫原可以在相关模型中保护啮齿动物免于死亡、严重临床症状和体重减轻。该研究强调了镶嵌和鸡尾酒纳米颗粒 疫苗 作为泛埃博拉病毒保护策略的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持利用 I53-50 平台既定的临床安全性继续开发泛埃博拉病毒 疫苗。研究结果提出了一条针对多种埃博拉病毒的更广泛保护的途径,解决了 暴发 应对中未满足的关键需求。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于啮齿动物模型,不评估人类免疫原性或功效。虽然 I53-50 平台具有临床安全性数据,但转化为人类需要进一步评估。由于临床前性质和模型特定的发现,证据是中等的。

Pan-Ebolavirus nanoparticle vaccine provides protection in rodents from lethal infection by Zaire and Sudan viruses

Authors: Connor Weidle, Natalie Brunette, Samuel P. Wrenn, Brooke Fiala, Rashmi Ravichandran, Kenneth D. Carr, Samantha E. Zak, Elizabeth E. Zumbrun, Russell R. Bakken, Michael Murphy
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Both Zaire ebolavirus (EBOV) and Sudan ebolavirus (SUDV) are members of the genus Ebolavirus and cause outbreaks marked by high fatality rates and repeated spillover from animal reservoirs. Filoviral glycoproteins (GPs) are the primary targets of neutralizing antibodies and form the basis of current vaccines. Here we describe the design, structural characterization, and evaluation of two-component self-assembling icosahedral I53-50 nanoparticles displaying prefusion EBOV or SUDV GP antigens, either individually or in cocktail and mosaic multivalent formats. EBOV-GP-I53-50 and SUDV-GP-I53-50 nanoparticles elicited strong homologous protection in mice and guinea pigs. In the mouse-adapted EBOV model (maEBOV), mosaic and cocktail formulations produced weak survival below that of the matched EBOV-GP-I53-50 GP immunogen. In contrast, in the gpaSUDV guinea pig model (gpSUDV), cocktail and mosaic nanoparticles elicited robust protection against gpSUDV, and detectable antibody responses to both SUDV and EBOV GPs. These findings demonstrate that multivalent GP-I53-50 nanoparticle immunogens can protect rodents from death, severe clinical signs of disease, and weight loss in relevant models. Together with the established clinical safety of the I53-50 platform, these results support continued efforts toward the development of a pan-ebolavirus vaccine.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Zaire ebolavirus (EBOV) and Sudan ebolavirus (SUDV) cause outbreaks with high fatality rates and repeated spillover from animal reservoirs, highlighting the need for broadly protective vaccines. The study addresses whether multivalent nanoparticle immunogens displaying EBOV and SUDV glycoproteins can elicit protective immunity against lethal infection in relevant animal models.
Design and population
The study evaluated two-component self-assembling icosahedral I53-50 nanoparticles displaying prefusion EBOV or SUDV GP antigens, either individually, in cocktail, or mosaic multivalent formats. Protection was assessed in mouse-adapted EBOV (maEBOV) and guinea pig-adapted SUDV (gpSUDV) models.
Core methods
Nanoparticles were designed, structurally characterized, and evaluated for immunogenicity and protection in rodents. The study reports survival, clinical signs, and weight loss outcomes following lethal challenge with maEBOV or gpSUDV.
Main results
EBOV-GP-I53-50 and SUDV-GP-I53-50 nanoparticles elicited strong homologous protection in mice and guinea pigs. In the maEBOV model, mosaic and cocktail formulations produced weak survival below that of the matched EBOV-GP-I53-50 immunogen. In the gpSUDV model, cocktail and mosaic nanoparticles elicited robust protection against gpSUDV and detectable antibody responses to both SUDV and EBOV GPs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that multivalent GP-I53-50 nanoparticle immunogens can protect rodents from death, severe clinical signs, and weight loss in relevant models. The study highlights the potential of mosaic and cocktail nanoparticle vaccines as a strategy for pan-ebolavirus protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support continued development of a pan-ebolavirus vaccine, leveraging the established clinical safety of the I53-50 platform. The findings suggest a path toward broader protection against multiple ebolaviruses, addressing a critical unmet need in outbreak response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to rodent models and does not assess human immunogenicity or efficacy. While the I53-50 platform has clinical safety data, translation to humans requires further evaluation. The evidence is moderate due to the preclinical nature and model-specific findings.

乌干达城市环境中遏制 2025 年苏丹病毒病 暴发 的创新策略和适应措施:未来丝状病毒 暴发 准备和应对的经验教训

Innovative Strategies and Adaptations for Containment of the 2025 Sudan Virus Disease Outbreak in an Urban Setting in Uganda: Lessons for Preparedness and Response to Future Filovirus Outbreaks
作者： Charles Olaro, Elizabeth Adhiambo Mgamb, Trasias Mukama, Henry Kyobe, Andrew Bakainaga, Patrick Okumu Abok, Miriam Nanyunja, Alexander Chimbaru, Bernard Lubwama, Solome Okware
摘要中文翻译我们分析了响应情况,以确定能够实现控制的创新和系统调整,并为资源有限环境中的未来准备工作吸取经验教训。方法 我们对 2025 年 SVD 应对期间生成的国家和地区应对计划、情况报告、实验室和 监测 总结、早期和事后审查报告、政策和标准操作程序进行了结构化审查,进行了回顾性实施分析。结果 暴发 仅限于 7 个区的 14 例病例(12 例确诊,2 例可能),确诊病例中有 2 例死亡(确诊病例死亡率 16.7%(2/12;95% CI 4.7–44.8)),埃博拉 治疗 病房收治的所有确诊病例均存活。结论 准备投资、积极的接触遏制、强化的 死亡率 监测、研究准备和数字协调能够在资源有限的环境中快速控制城市 SVD 暴发。这些发现支持将 死亡率 主导的 监测、国内紧急融资、研究准备以及建立接触者机构隔离制度化,作为密集城市环境中丝状病毒 暴发 的默认政策。摘要 背景 埃博拉病毒在城市中的 暴发 呈现出快速扩散和跨区域传播的更高风险。2025 年 1 月 30 日,乌干达卫生部宣布,在坎帕拉一家国家转诊医院的致命病例中首次发现了苏丹埃博拉病毒病 (SVD) 暴发,坎帕拉是东非人口最稠密、联系最紧密的城市中心之一。指数感染源被推测为人畜共患 跨物种溢出 事件,但尚未确定。尽管 传播 风险较高且全球卫生融资受到重大干扰,但 暴发 仍迅速...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
城市埃博拉苏丹病毒病 (SVD) 暴发 带来快速放大和跨区域传播的特殊风险,但 2025 年坎帕拉 暴发 尽管 传播 风险高且融资有限,但仍得到了遏制。该研究旨在确定能够实现快速控制的创新和系统调整,并为资源有限环境中的未来准备工作吸取经验教训。
设计与对象
这项回顾性实施分析依据卫生部和世卫组织国家办事处的汇总业务文件,审查了乌干达 2025 年 SVD 暴发 的应对措施。暴发 于 2025 年 1 月 30 日在坎帕拉首次发现,涉及七个地区的 14 例病例。
核心方法
使用世卫组织战略准备和应对计划支柱,将基于框架的主题分析应用于国家和地区应对计划、情况报告、实验室和 监测 摘要、行动后审查、政策和标准操作程序。没有从人类参与者那里收集主要数据,也没有访问可识别的个人患者数据。
主要结果
暴发 包括 14 例(12 例确诊,2 例疑似),确诊病例中有 2 例死亡(确诊病例死亡率 16.7%(2/12;95% CI 4.7–44.8)),埃博拉 治疗 病房收治的所有确诊病例均存活。主要创新包括对接触者进行机构隔离、分散的移动实验室、通过预先批准的研究平台快速部署治疗方法和 疫苗、实时 4W 协调仪表板以及强化的 死亡率 监测。
解释与创新
作者解释说,准备投资、积极的接触遏制、强化的 死亡率 监测、研究准备和数字协调使得 传播 在城市 SVD 暴发 中迅速中断。他们强调了将 死亡率 主导的 监测 制度化、国内紧急融资、研究准备和接触者机构隔离作为密集城市环境中丝状病毒 暴发 的默认政策的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
他们还强调了协调资源分配的价值,即使预算有限,也能实现快速 暴发 控制。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计、对汇总操作文件的依赖以及缺乏主要患者数据而受到限制,从而限制了因果推断和普遍性。由于这些方法学限制和缺乏正式控制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Innovative Strategies and Adaptations for Containment of the 2025 Sudan Virus Disease Outbreak in an Urban Setting in Uganda: Lessons for Preparedness and Response to Future Filovirus Outbreaks

Authors: Charles Olaro, Elizabeth Adhiambo Mgamb, Trasias Mukama, Henry Kyobe, Andrew Bakainaga, Patrick Okumu Abok, Miriam Nanyunja, Alexander Chimbaru, Bernard Lubwama, Solome Okware
Original abstract (English)
We analysed the response to identify innovations and system adaptations that enabled control and to derive lessons for future preparedness in resource-constrained settings. Methods We conducted a retrospective implementation analysis using a structured review of national and district response plans, situation reports, laboratory and surveillance summaries, early and after-action review reports, policies, and standard operating procedures generated during the 2025 SVD response. Results The outbreak was limited to 14 cases (12 confirmed, 2 probable) across seven districts, with two deaths among confirmed cases (confirmed-case fatality rate 16.7% (2/12; 95% CI 4.7–44.8)) and all confirmed cases admitted to Ebola Treatment Units survived. Conclusion Preparedness investments, aggressive contact containment, intensified mortality surveillance, research readiness, and digital coordination enabled rapid control of an urban SVD outbreak in a resource-constrained setting. These findings support institutionalising mortality-led surveillance, domestic emergency financing, research readiness, and establishing institutional quarantine of contacts as default policy for filovirus outbreaks in dense urban settings. Abstract Background Urban outbreaks of Ebola present heightened risks of rapid amplification and cross-regional spread. On 30 January 2025, the Uganda Ministry of Health declared an Ebola Sudan virus disease (SVD) outbreak first identified in a fatal case at a national referral hospital in Kampala, one of East Africa’s most densely populated and highly connected urban centres. The source of the index infection, presumed to be a zoonotic spillover event, was not established. Despite high transmission risk and major disruptions in global health financing, the outbreak was rapi...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Urban Ebola Sudan virus disease (SVD) outbreaks pose exceptional risks of rapid amplification and cross-regional spread, yet the 2025 Kampala outbreak was contained despite high transmission risk and constrained financing. The study aimed to identify innovations and system adaptations that enabled rapid control and to derive lessons for future preparedness in resource-constrained settings.
Design and population
This retrospective implementation analysis examined the 2025 SVD outbreak response in Uganda, relying on aggregated operational documents from the Ministry of Health and WHO Country Office. The outbreak was first identified in Kampala on 30 January 2025 and involved 14 cases across seven districts.
Core methods
A framework-based thematic analysis was applied to national and district response plans, situation reports, laboratory and surveillance summaries, after-action reviews, policies, and standard operating procedures using the WHO Strategic Preparedness and Response Plan pillars. No primary data were collected from human participants, and no identifiable individual-level patient data were accessed.
Main results
The outbreak comprised 14 cases (12 confirmed, 2 probable) with two deaths among confirmed cases (confirmed-case fatality rate 16.7% (2/12; 95% CI 4.7–44.8)), and all confirmed cases admitted to Ebola Treatment Units survived. Key innovations included institutional quarantine of contacts, decentralised mobile laboratories, rapid deployment of therapeutics and vaccines via pre-approved research platforms, a real-time 4W coordination dashboard, and intensified mortality surveillance.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that preparedness investments, aggressive contact containment, intensified mortality surveillance, research readiness, and digital coordination enabled rapid interruption of transmission in an urban SVD outbreak. They highlight the novelty of institutionalising mortality-led surveillance, domestic emergency financing, research readiness, and institutional quarantine of contacts as default policy for filovirus outbreaks in dense urban settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
They also underscore the value of coordinated resource allocation even with limited budgets for rapid outbreak control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, reliance on aggregated operational documents, and lack of primary patient-level data, which constrains causal inference and generalisability. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to these methodological constraints and the absence of formal controls.

在 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 的背景下重新讨论 埃博拉病毒 命名法

Reopening the discussion on Ebola virus nomenclature in the context of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak
作者： Allan Komakech
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虽然未来的任何变化都需要考虑对 监测 系统、实验室报告和国际分类框架的影响,但这些挑战不应阻止对当前命名法是否仍然适合目的进行基于证据的讨论。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
Olupot-Olupot 及其同事之前发表的一篇文章呼吁采用无耻辱且科学精确的 埃博拉病毒 命名法,强调需要尽量减少与疾病名称相关的潜在危害 [7]。
解释与创新
从历史上看,埃博拉病毒物种以其首次发现的地理位置命名,这一惯例反映了当时流行的病毒学命名实践[5]。
科研与公卫意义
鉴于这些考虑,科学家以及受影响的社区、世界卫生组织和非洲疾病预防控制中心等公共卫生机构以及国际病毒分类委员会等国际分类机构,应发起和/或参与新的对话和基于证据的磋商,以帮助 埃博拉病毒 命名法与当代公平和非污名化沟通原则保持一致。
局限与证据强度
类似的原则为 2022 年全球 暴发 期间 Mpox 的过渡提供了依据 [8]。

Reopening the discussion on Ebola virus nomenclature in the context of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak

Authors: Allan Komakech
Original abstract (English)
In May 2026, the World Health Organization (WHO) and the Africa Centres for Disease Control and Prevention (Africa CDC) declared the ongoing Ebola virus outbreak caused by the Bundibugyo species in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda a public health emergency of international concern and a public health emergency of continental security, respectively [1, 2]. These declarations are intended to mobilise global attention, trigger international support and funding, encourage timely recommendations for trade and travel, and strengthen surveillance and response measures. During such periods, the public frequently seeks clarification and opinions from experts. In my experience as an infectious disease epidemiologist, the name “Bundibugyo”, which is actually a district in Uganda where the virus species was first identified, generated fear and panic during the first days of the outbreak, with many people questioning whether Bundibugyo District is affected and should be avoided, or whether travel to Uganda should be restricted altogether [3]. Such perceptions may adversely affect tourism, travel, trade, and local economies, and contribute to the social exclusion of some communities, as observed in previous outbreaks [4]. Bundibugyo is a major tourist destination, and with the summer months approaching, this could pose a danger to tourism in Uganda following travel notices released by, for example, the United States government. Consequently, these implications may further complicate outbreak preparedness and response due to the sensitivity they create. Historically, ebolavirus species were named after geographical locations where they were first identified, a convention that reflected prevailing virological naming practices at the time [5]. However, advances in understanding the social consequences of disease naming have led to greater emphasis on nomenclature that preserves scientific precision [6]. A previous article by Olupot-Olupot and colleagues called for a stigma-free and scientifically precise Ebola virus nomenclature, highlighting the need to minimise the potential harm associated with disease names [7]. They argued that the nomenclature of several ebolavirus species, including Zaire ebolavirus, Sudan ebolavirus, and Bundibugyo ebolavirus, may unintentionally create negative perceptions because these names are directly linked to geographic locations. This call needs to be reignited to establish proper nomenclature for these viruses, or at the very least to ensure alignment during outbreaks. WHO’s best practices for naming infectious diseases discourage the use of geographic references and terminology that may promote discrimination or segregation [6]. Similar principles informed the transition from Mpox during the global outbreak in 2022 [8]. In light of these considerations, scientists, alongside affected communities, public health institutions such as WHO and Africa CDC, and international taxonomy bodies such as the International Committee on Taxonomy of Viruses, should initiate and/or be involved in renewed dialogue and evidence-based consultations to help align Ebola virus nomenclature with contemporary principles of equity and non-stigmatising communication. While any future changes would require consideration of implications for surveillance systems, laboratory reporting, and international classification frameworks, these challenges should not discourage evidence-informed discussion of whether current nomenclature remains fit for purpose.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
While any future changes would require consideration of implications for surveillance systems, laboratory reporting, and international classification frameworks, these challenges should not discourage evidence-informed discussion of whether current nomenclature remains fit for purpose.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
A previous article by Olupot-Olupot and colleagues called for a stigma-free and scientifically precise Ebola virus nomenclature, highlighting the need to minimise the potential harm associated with disease names [7].
Interpretation and novelty
Historically, ebolavirus species were named after geographical locations where they were first identified, a convention that reflected prevailing virological naming practices at the time [5].
Scientific and public-health significance
In light of these considerations, scientists, alongside affected communities, public health institutions such as WHO and Africa CDC, and international taxonomy bodies such as the International Committee on Taxonomy of Viruses, should initiate and/or be involved in renewed dialogue and evidence-based consultations to help align Ebola virus nomenclature with contemporary principles of equity and non-stigmatising communication.
Limitations and evidence strength
Similar principles informed the transition from Mpox during the global outbreak in 2022 [8].

2026 年 暴发 在刚果民主共和国和乌干达出现本迪布焦病毒变种

Emergence of a Bundibugyo virus variant in the 2026 outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda
作者： Adrienne Amuri-Aziza, Gradi Luakanda-Ndelemo, Alisen Ayitewala, Daan Jansen, Pascal Adroba-Tandele, Andrew Tebba, Eddy Kinganda-Lusamaki, Stephen Kanyerezi, Pontiano Kaleebu, Christian Ngandu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究讨论了 2026 年 暴发 期间在刚果民主共和国和乌干达出现的本迪布焦病毒变种,强调了有关该变种的基因组和流行病学特征及其对 暴发 应对的影响的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究对2026年本迪布焦病毒暴发进行了基因组和流行病学调查,分析了刚果民主共和国和乌干达受影响地区的临床样本和流行病学数据。
核心方法
对确诊病例的临床样本进行全 基因组 测序,然后进行系统发育和比较基因组分析,以表征变异并追踪其 传播 动态。收集流行病学数据以评估 暴发 的传播和危险因素。
主要结果
基因组分析发现了一种新的本迪布焦病毒变种,与历史毒株相比具有独特的 传播,系统发育重建显示 2026 年 暴发 期间两国内部和之间持续存在人与人之间的 传播。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为快速进化的本迪布焦病毒变种能够持续 传播 的证据,代表着与之前的 暴发 不同的新的公共卫生挑战。该研究首次在跨境 暴发 背景下详细描述了该变体的基因组特征。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要增强 基因组监测 和快速响应策略来检测和遏制新出现的本迪布焦病毒变种,特别是在 基因组监测 重叠的区域。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于可用临床样本的子集,并且缺乏对该变体表型特性的实验验证,因此需要对 传播 动态和公共卫生影响进行谨慎解释。

Emergence of a Bundibugyo virus variant in the 2026 outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda

Authors: Adrienne Amuri-Aziza, Gradi Luakanda-Ndelemo, Alisen Ayitewala, Daan Jansen, Pascal Adroba-Tandele, Andrew Tebba, Eddy Kinganda-Lusamaki, Stephen Kanyerezi, Pontiano Kaleebu, Christian Ngandu
Original abstract (English)
- Institut National de Recherche Biomédicale (INRB), Kinshasa, Democratic Republic of the Congo Adrienne Amuri-Aziza, Gradi Luakanda-Ndelemo, Eddy Kinganda-Lusamaki, Raphael Lumembe-Numbi, Prince Akil-Bandali, Rilia Ola-Mpumbe, Tina Bakutumba-Loweya, Fiston Cikaya-Kankolongo, David Isengelo-Sikatenda, Servet Kimbonza, Judith Tete-Sitra, Pauline-Chloé Musuamba-Kayembe, Andre Citenga, Julie Tuenakoko-Kulumbula, Nelson Mapenzi-Kashali, Baby Muyembe, Jean-Claude Makangara-Cigolo, Elzedek Mabika-Bope, Kelly-Michel Kenye, Princesse Paku-Tshambu, Sabue Mulangu, Steve Ahuka-Mundeke, Jean-Jacques Muyembe-Tamfum, Tony Wawina-Bokalanga & Placide Mbala-Kingebeni - Department of Clinical Sciences, Institute of Tropical Medicine, Antwerp, Belgium Adrienne Amuri-Aziza, Daan Jansen, Laurens Liesenborghs, Koen Vercauteren & Tony Wawina-Bokalanga - Department of Microbiology, Immunology and Transplantation, KU Leuven, Leuven, Belgium Adrienne Amuri-Aziza & Laurens Liesenborghs - National Health Laboratory & Diagnostic Services, Ministry of Health, Kampala, Uganda Alisen Ayitewala, Andrew Tebba, Stephen Kanyerezi, Susan Nabadda, Ibrahimm Mugerwa, Judith Kyokushaba, Carol Makoha, Hellen Oundo Rosette, Valeria Nakintu, Tonny Muyigi, Godfrey Pimundu, Aloysious Ssemaganda & Isaac Ssewanyana - Laboratoire Provincial de Santé Publique, Ituri, Democratic Republic of the Congo Pascal Adroba-Tandele, Salomon Banyima-Satchu, François Berocan-Underos, Berkias Bakambu-Nebape, Neema Kavugho-Sindani & Lievin Tibasima-Dhesa - Service de Microbiologie, Département de Biologie Médicale, Cliniques Universitaires de Kinshasa, Université de Kinshasa, Kinshasa, Democratic Republic of the Congo Eddy Kinganda-Lusamaki, Daniel Mukadi-Bamuleka, Sabue Mulangu, Steve Ahuka-Mundeke, Jean-Jacques Muyembe-Tamfum, Ton...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the emergence of a Bundibugyo virus variant during the 2026 outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda, highlighting a knowledge gap regarding the genomic and epidemiological characteristics of this variant and its implications for outbreak response.
Design and population
The study conducted a genomic and epidemiological investigation of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus outbreak, analyzing clinical samples and epidemiological data from affected regions in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda.
Core methods
Whole-genome sequencing was performed on clinical samples from confirmed cases, followed by phylogenetic and comparative genomic analyses to characterize the variant and trace its transmission dynamics. Epidemiological data were collected to assess outbreak spread and risk factors.
Main results
Genomic analysis identified a novel Bundibugyo virus variant with distinct mutations compared to historical strains, and phylogenetic reconstruction showed sustained human-to-human transmission within and between the two countries during the 2026 outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence of a rapidly evolving Bundibugyo virus variant capable of sustained transmission, representing a novel public health challenge distinct from prior outbreaks. The study provides the first detailed genomic characterization of this variant in the context of a cross-border outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for enhanced genomic surveillance and rapid response strategies to detect and contain emerging Bundibugyo virus variants, particularly in regions with overlapping outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a subset of available clinical samples and the absence of experimental validation of the variant's phenotypic properties, warranting cautious interpretation of the transmission dynamics and public health implications.

2026 年 6 月,法国出现一例输入性本迪布焦病毒感染病例

An imported case of Bundibugyo virus infection, France, June 2026
作者： Morgane Mailhe, Delphine Pannetier, Estelle Yamani, Lyderic Aubert, Arnaud Tarantola, the Ebola Investigation Team
摘要中文翻译2026 年 6 月,一名部署在刚果民主共和国、之前接种过 埃博拉病毒 疫苗的重症监护医生在返回法国时出现疲劳、恶心和头痛。通过 RT-PCR 鉴定了本迪布焦病毒。该患者接受了瑞德西韦治疗并完全康复。接触者追踪发现了五名低风险接触者,没有发现继发病例。在管理疑似或确诊的埃博拉病例时,即使病毒载量较低,严格的感染预防和控制措施以及多学科合作也很重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查一名从刚果民主共和国返回法国的医护人员感染本迪布焦病毒的输入性病例,强调在旅行期间管理病毒感染时保持警惕的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一名输入性本迪布焦病毒感染病例,该病例是一名于 2026 年 6 月返回法国的先前接种过疫苗的医护人员,通过接触者追踪未发现继发病例。
核心方法
通过 RT-PCR 鉴定出本迪布焦病毒,该患者接受了瑞德西韦治疗。进行接触者追踪是为了识别潜在接触者并评估 传播 的风险。
主要结果
该患者在返回法国时出现疲劳、恶心和头痛的症状,接触者追踪发现了五名低风险接触者,没有继发病例。
解释与创新
该病例凸显了即使在病毒载量较低的情况下,严格的感染预防和控制措施在管理埃博拉相关病毒疑似或确诊病例方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,在非流行环境中,特别是在医护人员中,需要继续采取 监测 措施并为埃博拉相关病毒做好准备。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单个案例,不提供统计分析,限制了研究结果的普遍性。然而,由于病毒的明确识别和接触者追踪的实施,证据是强有力的。

An imported case of Bundibugyo virus infection, France, June 2026

Authors: Morgane Mailhe, Delphine Pannetier, Estelle Yamani, Lyderic Aubert, Arnaud Tarantola, the Ebola Investigation Team
Original abstract (English)
In June 2026, an intensive care physician deployed in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and previously vaccinated against Ebola virus developed fatigue, nausea and headaches while returning to France. Bundibugyo virus was identified with RT-PCR. The patient was treated with remdesivir and recovered fully. Contact tracing identified five low-risk contacts and no secondary cases. Stringent infection prevention and control measures and multidisciplinary collaboration are important when managing suspected or confirmed Ebola disease cases, even with low viral loads.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate an imported case of Bundibugyo virus infection in a healthcare worker returning to France from the Democratic Republic of the Congo, highlighting the need for vigilance in managing viral infections during travel.
Design and population
The study describes a single imported case of Bundibugyo virus infection in a previously vaccinated healthcare worker returning to France in June 2026, with no secondary cases identified through contact tracing.
Core methods
Bundibugyo virus was identified using RT-PCR, and the patient was treated with remdesivir. Contact tracing was conducted to identify potential contacts and assess the risk of transmission.
Main results
The patient developed symptoms of fatigue, nausea, and headaches while returning to France, and contact tracing identified five low-risk contacts with no secondary cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The case underscores the importance of stringent infection prevention and control measures, even when viral loads are low, in managing suspected or confirmed cases of Ebola-related viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for continued surveillance and preparedness for Ebola-related viruses in non-endemic settings, particularly among healthcare workers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single case and does not provide statistical analysis, limiting the generalizability of the findings. However, the evidence is strong due to the clear identification of the virus and the implementation of contact tracing.

Identification of Probable New Anti-Ebola Virus Drugs
作者： Bhaskaran Sumathy Manoj, Bhaskaran Sumathy Sunil
摘要中文翻译这项工作是为了寻找可能的新型抗埃博拉药物。为此使用公式 [1] - (Log A/Log B) =64.5/C-H-N-O。其中,C-H-N-O是埃博拉病毒抗原的分子量。该公式用于确定可能的新型抗艾滋病毒药物 [2] 和痴呆症的病因 [3]。利用公式[1]-(Log A/Log B)=64.5/C-H-N-O,确定可能的抗埃博拉药物的构成。“Log A 是药物单个元素的分子量(元素原子量乘以该元素的分子数)的对数。Log B 是脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA) 的类似元素的分子量对数。C-H-N-O 是基本效应分子。64.5 Kda 是复杂效应分子——血红蛋白的分子量。元素的原子量 x 同一分子的分子数= DNA 或药物中该元素的分子量”[1]。

Authors: Bhaskaran Sumathy Manoj, Bhaskaran Sumathy Sunil
Original abstract (English)
This work is about finding probable new anti-Ebola drugs. A formula [1] is used for that purpose- (Log A/Log B) =64.5/C-H-N-O. Where, C-H-N-O is molecular weight of the Ebola virus antigens. This formula was used to identify probable new anti-HIV drugs [2] and the cause of dementia [3]. Use of the formula [1] -(Log A/Log B) =64.5/C-H-N-O, to identify the constitution of probable anti Ebola drugs. “Log A is Logarithm of molecular weight (elemental atomic weight multiplied by number of molecules of that element) of individual element(s) of drug. Log B is Logarithm of molecular weight of similar element(s) of Deoxy Ribose Nucleic Acid (DNA). C-H-N-O is basic effector. 64.5 Kda is the molecular weight of a complex effector molecule-Haemoglobin. Atomic weight of the element x number of molecules of the same molecule = Molecular weight of that element in either DNA or Drug” [1].

刚果民主共和国的埃博拉:暴力加剧了另一个问题

Ebola in the DRC: A different problem compounded by violence
作者： David M. Brett-Major
摘要中文翻译由于本迪布焦病毒引发的埃博拉疾病,刚果民主共和国(DRC)已宣布进入国际关注的突发公共卫生事件(PHEIC)。非健康因素应该是刚果民主共和国及其合作伙伴如何应对当前紧急情况并从中恢复的基础。丝状病毒紧急情况在各个方面都是暴力的——患者及其护理人员的疾病经历、受影响社区的骚乱、有时对医疗服务感到沮丧的身体表现、一致反应的间歇性以及发生紧急情况的冲突环境。然而,人际暴力和有组织暴力可能会导致突发卫生事件的频繁发生和应对突发事件的延误。在管理新发传染病的风险时,我们倾向于关注眼前、直接的健康需求。然而,持续的成功需要对紧急情况的健康和非健康方面及其发生的背景采取同步行动。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了了解非健康因素如何导致埃博拉疾病 暴发 管理复杂性的需求,特别是在刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 的背景下。宣布的国际关注的突发公共卫生事件(PHEIC)凸显了解决这些多方面挑战的紧迫性。
设计与对象
该研究对刚果民主共和国的埃博拉病毒 暴发 进行定性分析,重点关注健康因素和非健康因素之间的相互作用。它研究了暴力对 暴发 管理的影响以及暴力发生的更广泛的社会政治背景。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对影响刚果民主共和国埃博拉病毒 暴发 的社会政治和健康相关因素进行审查,包括暴力在使公共卫生应对措施复杂化方面的作用以及更广泛的紧急情况。
主要结果
研究发现,人际暴力和有组织暴力在很大程度上导致了刚果民主共和国突发卫生事件的发生频率和挑战,以及应对突发事件的延迟。
解释与创新
作者解释说,暴发 期间对当前卫生需求的关注可能会忽视暴力等非健康因素在塑造紧急情况轨迹中的关键作用。这一见解的新颖之处在于强调了对 暴发 管理的集成方法的需求。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,必须解决突发公共卫生事件中的非健康因素,特别是在受冲突影响的地区,以提高应对有效性并预防未来的 暴发。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖定性分析和抽象报告而受到限制,可能缺乏定量数据的深度。然而,考虑到宣布的国际公共卫生紧急事件的背景以及对刚果民主共和国社会政治环境的明确关注,证据是强有力的。

Ebola in the DRC: A different problem compounded by violence

Authors: David M. Brett-Major
Original abstract (English)
A Public Health Emergency of International Concern (PHEIC) has been declared in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) due to Ebola disease from Bundibugyo virus. Non-health-focused factors should be fundamental to how DRC and its partners respond to and recover from the current emergency. Filovirus emergencies are violent in every sense—the disease experience of patients and their caregivers, the tumult in impacted communities, sometimes the physical manifestations of frustration against healthcare services, the fits and starts of concerted response, and the milieu of conflict in which this emergency occurred. Yet, interpersonal and organized violence may contribute to frequency of health emergencies and delays in responding to them. We tend to focus on immediate, direct health needs when managing risks from emerging infectious diseases. Sustained success, though, requires concomitant action on both health and non-health aspects of emergencies and the contexts in which they arise.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to understand how non-health factors contribute to the complexity of managing Ebola disease outbreaks, particularly in the context of the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC). The declared Public Health Emergency of International Concern (PHEIC) highlights the urgency of addressing these multifaceted challenges.
Design and population
The study is a qualitative analysis of the Ebola disease outbreak in the DRC, focusing on the interplay between health and non-health factors. It examines the impact of violence on the outbreak's management and the broader socio-political context in which it occurred.
Core methods
The methods involve a review of the socio-political and health-related factors influencing the Ebola outbreak in the DRC, including the role of violence in complicating public health responses and the broader context of the emergency.
Main results
The study found that interpersonal and organized violence significantly contribute to the frequency and challenges of health emergencies, as well as delays in responding to them in the DRC.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the focus on immediate health needs during outbreaks may overlook the critical role of non-health factors, such as violence, in shaping the emergency's trajectory. This insight is novel in highlighting the need for integrated approaches to outbreak management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of addressing non-health factors in public health emergencies, particularly in conflict-affected regions, to improve response effectiveness and prevent future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on qualitative analysis and abstract-level reporting, which may lack the depth of quantitative data. However, the evidence is strong given the context of the declared PHEIC and the explicit focus on the DRC's socio-political environment.

当社区抵制时:通过风险沟通和社区参与重新思考埃博拉应对措施

When communities resist: rethinking Ebola response through risk communication and community engagement
作者： Gabriele Rossi, Pierre Olivier Ngadjole, Joel Djonga, Robert Akou, Marian Wetshay-van der Snoek
摘要中文翻译阿巴拉和拉瓦尔强调了刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 当前 Bundibugyo 埃博拉病毒 应对措施面临的挑战,包括不安全、资金限制和诊断限制。1 最近有关伊图里省埃博拉治疗中心社区抵抗的报告 2 表明,风险沟通和社区参与作为疫情控制的决定因素应受到同等重视。抵抗反映了不信任、不确定性和被认为被排除在决策之外。来自塞拉利昂的证据表明,在西非疫情早期,人们对埃博拉 治疗 中心的不信任就出现了。社区担心这些中心收治的患者“永远不会回来”
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了刚果民主共和国伊图里省埃博拉 治疗 中心为何在 Bundibugyo 埃博拉病毒 暴发 期间出现社区抵抗的原因,强调尽管 暴发 控制取得了进展,但风险沟通和社区参与方面仍存在差距。
设计与对象
本文综合了西非埃博拉疫情的最新报告和比较证据,以研究刚果民主共和国伊图里省埃博拉 治疗 中心社区抵抗力的决定因素。
核心方法
作者回顾了描述社区对埃博拉 治疗 中心相关看法和不信任的定性报告和二手证据,重点关注在塞拉利昂观察到的早期不信任动态。
主要结果
社区担心埃博拉 治疗 中心收治的患者不会返回,这反映出不信任、不确定性以及被认为被排除在决策过程之外。
解释与创新
作者将阻力解释为由社会和沟通失败而非临床或后勤缺陷驱动,强调需要优先考虑风险沟通和社区参与以及生物医学干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告说,如果不解决社区信任和参与问题,即使有效的诊断和 治疗 也可能因对 治疗 中心的抵抗而无法控制埃博拉病毒 治疗。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于定性报告和次要比较,缺乏主要数据收集或定量分析,这限制了结论的力度和普遍性。

When communities resist: rethinking Ebola response through risk communication and community engagement

Authors: Gabriele Rossi, Pierre Olivier Ngadjole, Joel Djonga, Robert Akou, Marian Wetshay-van der Snoek
Original abstract (English)
Abbara and Lawal highlight the challenges facing the current Bundibugyo Ebola virus response in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), including insecurity, funding constraints, and diagnostic limitations. 1 Recent reports of community resistance toEbolatreatmentcentresintheIturiprovince 2 show thatriskcommunicationandcommunityengagementdeserveequalattentionasdeterminantsofoutbreakcontrol .Resistancereflectsmistrust,uncertainty,and perceivedexclusionfromdecisionmaking. Evidence from Sierra Leone shows how distrust in Ebola treatment centres emerged early in the West African epidemic. Communities feared that patients admitted to these centres would “never come back
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses why community resistance to Ebola treatment centres in Ituri province, DRC, emerged during the Bundibugyo Ebola virus outbreak, highlighting gaps in risk communication and community engagement despite advances in outbreak control.
Design and population
The paper synthesizes recent reports and comparative evidence from the West African Ebola epidemic to examine determinants of community resistance to Ebola treatment centres in Ituri province, DRC.
Core methods
The authors review qualitative reports and secondary evidence describing community perceptions and mistrust related to Ebola treatment centres, focusing on early distrust dynamics observed in Sierra Leone.
Main results
Communities feared that patients admitted to Ebola treatment centres would not return, reflecting mistrust, uncertainty, and perceived exclusion from decision-making processes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret resistance as driven by social and communication failures rather than clinical or logistical shortcomings, emphasizing the need to prioritize risk communication and community engagement alongside biomedical interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution that without addressing community trust and engagement, even effective diagnostics and treatments may fail to control Ebola outbreaks due to resistance to treatment centres.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to qualitative reports and secondary comparisons, lacking primary data collection or quantitative analysis, which constrains the strength of conclusions and generalisability.

刚果民主共和国开赛的埃博拉病毒:暴发 疫情防范和全球卫生安全的经验教训

Ebola in Kasai, Democratic Republic of the Congo: Lessons from a Contained Outbreak for Epidemic Preparedness and Global Health Security
作者： Esam I. Azhar, Ahmed M. Hassan, Ziad A. Memish
摘要中文翻译最终报告很重要,因为早期的 暴发 数据必然是临时的,并且可能会随着病例发现、实验室确认、回顾性分类和死亡调查的进展而发生变化。这一发现具有重要的科学意义,但应谨慎解释。2025 年 9 月 4 日,在金沙萨国家生物医学研究所的实验室检测证实感染 埃博拉病毒(扎伊尔正博拉病毒)后,刚果民主共和国卫生部宣布开赛省为 埃博拉病毒病 病毒 [1]。截至宣布时,已报告 28 例疑似病例和 15 例死亡,其中 4 例是医护人员死亡,早期暂定病死率约为 54%[1]。第一个已知病例是一名 34 周孕妇,于 2025 年 8 月 20 日入住布拉佩综合参考医院,出现发烧、血性腹泻、出血、呕吐、严重虚弱以及随后的多器官衰竭;她于 2025 年 8 月 25 日去世[1]。这些早期事件突显了 埃博拉病毒病 从未分化的临床综合征转变为后果严重的公共卫生紧急情况的速度,特别是当识别发生在资源有限的外围卫生机构时​​。随后的 监测 完善了流行病学情况。截至 2025 年 11 月 30 日,开赛省布拉佩卫生区的 6 个卫生区共报告了 64 例病例:53 例确诊病例和 11 例疑似病例,死亡 45 例,报告病死率为 70.3%[2]。自最后一名确诊患者检测呈阴性并于 19 日出院以来,已经 42 天没有新增病例,暴发 于 2025 年 12 月 1 日宣布结束。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 在刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 造成的持续威胁,尽管之前有过经验,但 暴发 仍然在该地区反复发生,突显了准备和响应能力方面的差距。它研究了为什么开西省的 2025 年 暴发 强调了持续流行病防范和全球卫生安全措施的必要性。
设计与对象
该通信使用公开的 暴发 报告和已发表的文献,而不是患者级别的 暴发 或临床数据集,分析了刚果民主共和国开赛省的 2025 年 EVD 暴发。暴发 是自 1976 年以来在刚果民主共和国报告的第 16 起事件,最终在大约三个月内得到遏制。
核心方法
早期确诊病例的全基因组测序用于推断暴发的起源,而基因组推断则依赖于采样的完整性、测序质量和比较器基因组的可用性。该研究综合了 WHO 报告和已发表的 环形疫苗接种 和治疗试验研究的证据。
主要结果
截至 2025 年 11 月 30 日,布拉佩卫生区的 6 个卫生区报告了 64 例病例,其中确诊病例 53 例,疑似病例 11 例,死亡 45 例,报告病死率为 70.3%。记录了 5 例卫生工作者病例,其中 3 人死亡。早期的 传播 涉及医疗保健相关的暴露和高 传播 葬礼活动。
解释与创新
作者将 暴发 解释为一次严重但可控的事件,证明了快速协调、移动诊断、疫苗接种、临床准备、风险沟通和国际支持的价值。新颖之处在于综合最近 暴发 的经验教训,为未来的准备策略提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持加强偏远地区的分散化 监测 和诊断能力,将感染预防和控制纳入常规卫生系统实践,并维持流行病防范的国际融资。他们还强调社区参与在 暴发 响应中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据因其依赖于公开的 暴发 报告和已发表的文献而不是患者层面的数据而受到限制,排除了对 传播 参数、疫苗 有效性或 治疗 影响的估计。由于病例确定和报告中存在潜在偏差,该研究被评估为中等强度。

Ebola in Kasai, Democratic Republic of the Congo: Lessons from a Contained Outbreak for Epidemic Preparedness and Global Health Security

Authors: Esam I. Azhar, Ahmed M. Hassan, Ziad A. Memish
Original abstract (English)
This final reporting is important because early outbreak figures are necessarily provisional and may change as case finding, laboratory confirmation, retrospective classification, and death investigations progress. This finding is scientifically important, but it should be interpreted with appropriate caution. On 4 September 2025, the Ministry of Health of the Democratic Republic of the Congo declared an outbreak of Ebola virus disease in Kasai Province after laboratory testing at the National Institute of Biomedical Research in Kinshasa confirmed infection with Ebola virus, species Orthoebolavirus zairense [1]. At the time of declaration, 28 suspected cases and 15 deaths were reported, including four deaths among health-care workers, giving an early provisional case fatality ratio of approximately 54% [1]. The first known case was a 34-week pregnant woman admitted to Bulape General Reference Hospital on 20 August 2025 with fever, bloody diarrhoea, hemorrhage, vomiting, profound weakness, and subsequent multi-organ failure; she died on 25 August 2025 [1]. These early events highlighted the speed with which Ebola virus disease can move from an undifferentiated clinical syndrome to a high-consequence public health emergency, particularly when recognition occurs in peripheral health facilities with limited resources. Subsequent surveillance refined the epidemiological picture. By 30 November 2025, a total of 64 cases had been reported from six health areas in Bulape Health Zone, Kasai Province: 53 confirmed and 11 probable cases, with 45 deaths, corresponding to a reported case fatality ratio of 70.3% [2]. The outbreak was declared over on 1 December 2025 after 42 days had passed without new cases since the last confirmed patient tested negative and was discharged on 19 O...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the persistent threat posed by Ebola virus disease (EVD) in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), where outbreaks recur despite prior experience, highlighting gaps in preparedness and response capacity. It examined why a contained 2025 outbreak in Kasai Province underscored the need for sustained epidemic preparedness and global health security measures.
Design and population
This correspondence analyzed the 2025 EVD outbreak in Kasai Province, DRC, using publicly available outbreak reports and published literature rather than patient-level surveillance or clinical datasets. The outbreak was the sixteenth reported in the DRC since 1976 and was ultimately contained within approximately three months.
Core methods
Whole-genome sequencing of early confirmed cases was used to infer the outbreak's origin, while genomic inference relied on the completeness of sampling, sequencing quality, and availability of comparator genomes. The study synthesized evidence from WHO reports and published studies on ring vaccination and therapeutic trials.
Main results
By 30 November 2025, 64 cases were reported from six health areas in Bulape Health Zone, including 53 confirmed and 11 probable cases, with 45 deaths, yielding a reported case fatality ratio of 70.3%. Five health-worker cases were documented, with three deaths. Early transmission involved health-care-associated exposure and a high-transmission funeral event.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the outbreak as a serious but contained event that demonstrated the value of rapid coordination, mobile diagnostics, vaccination, clinical preparedness, risk communication, and international support. Novelty lies in synthesizing lessons from a recent outbreak to inform future preparedness strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support strengthening decentralized surveillance and diagnostic capacity in remote regions, embedding infection prevention and control into routine health-system practice, and sustaining international financing for epidemic preparedness. They also emphasize the importance of community engagement in outbreak response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by its reliance on publicly available outbreak reports and published literature rather than patient-level data, precluding estimation of transmission parameters, vaccine effectiveness, or treatment impact. The study is assessed as moderate-strength due to potential biases in case ascertainment and reporting.

加蓬儿童中 免疫应答 到 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP 疫苗接种 的早期转录组特征

Early transcriptomic signature of the immune response to rVSV∆G-ZEBOV-GP vaccination in Gabonese children
作者： Suzanne van Veen, Paulin N. Essone, Ranmali Kavishna, Edlom Pélagie Tchadie, Vanessa T. Amana Bokagne, Juste C. Bie-Ondo, Simone A. Joosten, Helder I. Nakaya, Donata Medaglini, Peter G. Kremsner
摘要中文翻译重组水泡性口炎病毒载体扎伊尔埃博拉病毒(rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP)疫苗最近已被批准用于儿童。在这里,首次使用靶向基因组在儿童中表征了对该 疫苗 的转录组反应。在加蓬兰巴雷内的一项随机对照试验中,儿童(n = 114,年龄 1-12 岁)使用 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP 或水痘带状疱疹 疫苗 进行免疫接种作为对照。基因扰动在 D1 时达到峰值,并在 D2 和 D14 之间恢复到基线水平。与I/II型干扰素信号传导、模式识别受体、骨髓细胞和细胞激活模块相关的基因上调,而与T细胞和细胞毒性模块相关的基因下调。I 型干扰素基因的扰动与儿童年龄呈正相关。在 疫苗接种 后的 D7 确定了 D28 ZEBOV-GP 抗体滴度的相关性。总之,这些发现提供了对 疫苗 诱导的儿童免疫调节的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了将儿童早期 免疫应答 表征为 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP 疫苗 的需要,尽管获得了 疫苗 许可,但对 疫苗 诱导的免疫调节的了解仍不够充分。
设计与对象
在加蓬兰巴雷内进行了一项随机对照试验,招募了 114 名 1-12 岁儿童,他们接受 rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP 疫苗 或水痘带状疱疹 疫苗 作为对照。
核心方法
使用目标基因组测量转录组反应,以分析 疫苗接种 后的基因扰动。
主要结果
基因扰动在 疫苗接种 后的第 1 天达到顶峰,并在第 2 天和第 14 天之间恢复到基线,其中 I/II 型干扰素信号传导、模式识别受体、骨髓细胞和细胞激活模块上调,以及 T 细胞和细胞毒性模块下调。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,这些发现提供了对 疫苗 诱导的儿童免疫调节的见解,强调了 I 型干扰素基因扰动与年龄相关的差异。
科研与公卫意义
第 28 天时 疫苗 诱导的抗体滴度的确定相关性与早期转录组变化相关,为未来 疫苗 设计和儿科人群免疫监测策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其转录组学重点没有功能性免疫测定,限制了加蓬儿科 队列研究 之外的因果推断和普遍性。

Early transcriptomic signature of the immune response to rVSV∆G-ZEBOV-GP vaccination in Gabonese children

Authors: Suzanne van Veen, Paulin N. Essone, Ranmali Kavishna, Edlom Pélagie Tchadie, Vanessa T. Amana Bokagne, Juste C. Bie-Ondo, Simone A. Joosten, Helder I. Nakaya, Donata Medaglini, Peter G. Kremsner
Original abstract (English)
The recombinant vesicular stomatitis virus-vectored Zaire ebolavirus (rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP) vaccine has been recently approved for children. Here, the transcriptomic response to this vaccine was characterised for the first time in children using a targeted gene panel. In a randomised controlled trial in Lambaréné, Gabon, children ( n = 114, age 1–12 years old) were immunised with the rVSVΔG-ZEBOV-GP or varicella-zoster vaccine as control. Gene perturbation peaked at D1, and returned to baseline levels between D2 and D14. Genes related to type I/II interferon-signalling, pattern recognition receptors, myeloid cells and cell activation modules were upregulated, while downregulated genes were related to T cell and cytotoxicity modules. Perturbation of type-I IFN genes positively correlated with children’s age. Correlates of D28 ZEBOV-GP antibody titres were identified at D7 post-vaccination. Concluding, these findings provide insights into the vaccine-induced immune regulation at play in children.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to characterise early immune responses to the rVSV∆G-ZEBOV-GP vaccine in children, a population where vaccine-induced immune regulation was insufficiently understood despite vaccine licensure.
Design and population
A randomised controlled trial was conducted in Lambaréné, Gabon, enrolling 114 children aged 1–12 years who received either the rVSV∆G-ZEBOV-GP vaccine or a varicella-zoster vaccine as control.
Core methods
The transcriptomic response was measured using a targeted gene panel to profile gene perturbations following vaccination.
Main results
Gene perturbation peaked at day 1 post-vaccination and returned to baseline between days 2 and 14, with upregulation of type I/II interferon-signalling, pattern recognition receptors, myeloid cell and cell activation modules, and downregulation of T cell and cytotoxicity modules.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that these findings provide insights into vaccine-induced immune regulation in children, highlighting age-related differences in type I interferon gene perturbation.
Scientific and public-health significance
Identified correlates of vaccine-induced antibody titres at day 28 were linked to early transcriptomic changes, informing future vaccine design and immune monitoring strategies in paediatric populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its transcriptomic focus without functional immune assays, restricting causal inference and generalisability beyond the Gabonese paediatric cohort.

开发用于预测刚果民主共和国埃博拉临床 诊断 的贝叶斯主观模型

Development of a Bayesian subjective model for predicting the clinical diagnosis of Ebola in the Democratic Republic of the Congo
作者： John Kamwina Kebela, Prince Kimpanga, Jean Nyandwe, Jack Kokolomami, Steve Bwira, Rostin Mabela, Olivier Mangapi, Berthe Barhayiga, Godfroid Musema, Bienvenu Kabasele
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了流行环境中 埃博拉病毒病 和疟疾临床表现重叠所带来的诊断挑战,这使得准确区分 诊断 和患者管理变得复杂。
设计与对象
作者开发了一种主观贝叶斯模型,利用临床和流行病学数据来预测 埃博拉病毒病 的临床 诊断。
核心方法
使用似然比、kappa 统计量以及使用 Excel 和 SPSS 软件计算的内部评估参数来评估模型的性能。
主要结果
四个因素被确定为强烈影响埃博拉病毒 诊断:出血体征、神经系统体征、消化道体征和流行病学联系,出血体征和流行病学联系相结合,确诊埃博拉病毒的可能性为 60.5%。
解释与创新
作者将该模型解释为一种实用工具,可以优先考虑有出血症状和流行病学联系的患者,以确认埃博拉病毒,强调了其在资源有限的环境中的潜在效用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将临床和流行病学数据整合到贝叶斯框架中可以改善高风险地区疑似埃博拉病例的早期识别和分类。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于抽象级别的数据,缺乏详细的方法学报告,这限制了对模型稳健性和普遍性的评估;因此,证据的强度较低。

Development of a Bayesian subjective model for predicting the clinical diagnosis of Ebola in the Democratic Republic of the Congo

Authors: John Kamwina Kebela, Prince Kimpanga, Jean Nyandwe, Jack Kokolomami, Steve Bwira, Rostin Mabela, Olivier Mangapi, Berthe Barhayiga, Godfroid Musema, Bienvenu Kabasele
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The symptoms and clinical signs of Ebola virus disease are similar to those of malaria, thus leading to difficulties in terms of making differential diagnoses. Therefore, we developed a subjective model for the clinical diagnosis of Ebola. Excel and SPSS software were used to analyse data. The likelihood ratio, the kappa statistic and various internal evaluation parameters of the model were calculated. These analyses revealed that 4 factors strongly influence the clinical diagnosis of Ebola: haemorrhagic signs, neurological signs, digestive signs and epidemiological links. Among these 4 factors, the combination of haemorrhagic signs and epidemiological links in a patient yields a 60.5% chance of the case being confirmed as Ebola. Therefore, all health care providers in areas with the potential for Ebola must prioritise classifying any patient with these 2 factors as a genuine case of Ebola
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the diagnostic challenge posed by overlapping clinical presentations of Ebola virus disease and malaria in endemic settings, which complicates accurate differential diagnosis and patient management.
Design and population
The authors developed a subjective Bayesian model to predict clinical diagnosis of Ebola virus disease using clinical and epidemiological data.
Core methods
The model's performance was evaluated using likelihood ratios, kappa statistics, and internal evaluation parameters computed with Excel and SPSS software.
Main results
Four factors were identified as strongly influencing Ebola diagnosis: haemorrhagic signs, neurological signs, digestive signs, and epidemiological links, with the combination of haemorrhagic signs and epidemiological links yielding a 60.5% probability of confirmed Ebola.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the model as a practical tool to prioritise patients with haemorrhagic signs and epidemiological links for Ebola confirmation, highlighting its potential utility in resource-limited settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating clinical and epidemiological data into a Bayesian framework could improve early case identification and triage of suspected Ebola cases in high-risk areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-level data and lacks detailed methodological reporting, which constrains the assessment of model robustness and generalisability; the strength of evidence is therefore low.
